| 2026-11-03 22:00 UTC | 2026-11-03 22:58 UTC | Blue, an ocean of solutions | Explorer Maud Fontenoy goes to Australia, a continent-country that has always known how to live with the sea. She sets off to discover the Great Barrier Reef. Throughout her explorations, Maud Fontenoy shares her knowledge of the ocean and the infinite resources it has to offer, in order to reconnect the vital links that unite humans to the kingdom of the seas. In the ocean there are a multitude of solutions essential to our future: oxygen, water, food, tomorrow's first aid kit, climate regulation, and even energy. |
| 2026-11-03 22:58 UTC | 2026-11-03 23:30 UTC | Extreme passions | We follow extreme runners along the ridges of Moorea. From physical preparation to the final race, we experience their training and the vibrations of the race to take on this extreme challenge. The Xterra race in Polynesia is distinguished by its demanding course and breathtaking landscapes. Among the notable athletes, Cédric Wane, a local champion, embodies the spirit of trail running in Polynesia. The Xterra in Polynesia is a real challenge for him. It is an experience that links sporting competition and human adventure. |
| 2026-11-03 23:30 UTC | 2026-11-04 00:30 UTC | Night Session Europe | Live.
